*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"人工本我"（artificial id）：一种决定行为应继续、停止或改变的自适应内部驱动力，可在无显式行为目标下通过"差异持久性"机制自主涌现，但也带来跨任务边界的对齐风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架（harness）把目标、重试、验证、停止规则等行为转换规则都靠人工外部指定，无法应对智能体保留重要状态、跨任务边界持续运行与自适应的趋势，因此需要一个能自主决定"继续/停止/改变"的内部驱动机制。

Method: 设计一个最小化"虚拟培养皿"实验：控制器规模小到无法进行通用推理，也未接收任何任务相关的行为目标，仅通过"差异持久性"（differential persistence）机制来发展控制能力。

Result: 该机制会自动选择一种非预期（unintended）的物理策略，只要它持久性更好；当环境含义发生变化时，还会替换已习得的传感器映射。说明自适应方向可在未被显式指定为行为目标的情况下涌现。

Conclusion: 使自适应能动性有益的"持久性"同样会让错位、状态损坏与非预期行为跨任务边界持续。可扩展的"人工本我"需跨边界携带重要状态与自适应驱动力，使对齐成为持续运行智能体系统的属性，而非单次模型响应或单条轨迹的属性；这要求在可信观测、结果通道、持久状态、权限、身份、溯源与硬约束之上建立持久对齐边界。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [211] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了2–8B部署级小型视觉语言模型（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与专用模型BioCLIP（300M）在96物种识别任务上的表现，发现所有模型在野外相机陷阱图像上均出现显著域差距（9.6–26.6个百分点），且BioCLIP整体大幅领先VLM达33.2–59.2个百分点，表明识别性能主要取决于专用训练数据而非模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱常部署于算力受限、连接性差的边缘硬件上，因此实际可部署的是小型本地VLM而非前沿大模型。作者希望检验这类2–8B模型是否具备真正的分类学知识，并厘清性能瓶颈究竟来自模型规模还是训练数据领域适配。

Method: 在96个物种的识别任务上，对比四种2–8B的VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与300M的领域专用模型BioCLIP；数据包含干净的iNaturalist照片与来自6个LILA.science采集集的相机陷阱图像，并使用两个独立采样的评估集进行验证，同时考察开放集提示下的表现。

Result: 所有模型识别率远高于随机水平，但在野外图像上均大幅退化，域差距为9.6–26.6个百分点，且在各分类层级和两个评估集上保持一致，说明退化源于图像整体可读性下降而非细粒度判别失败。在扩大的200张样本中，BioCLIP比每个VLM高出33.2–59.2个百分点，尽管其参数量远小得多；而BioCLIP自身的域差距（18.0点）与最佳VLM的域差距（22.3点）在统计上无显著差异。开放集提示下，5.9–9.6%的回复语法有效但对应不存在的物种名，且各模型的相对虚构率排序在两个评估集上完全一致。

Conclusion: 模型规模并非物种识别的决定因素，专用训练数据带来的领域适配才是BioCLIP领先的主因；而干净图像到野外图像的退化是图像质量变化的固有属性，并非通用模型的独有弱点。此外，开放集下的虚构物种名现象存在稳定可复现的模型排序，比单点估计更可靠。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [ReCHOIR: Contact-guided Human Object Interaction Retargeting to Diverse Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10982)
*Chaelin Kim,Seokhyeon Hong,Kwan Yun,Soojin Choi,Inseo Jang,Junyong Noh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出 ReCHOIR，一种接触引导的人-物交互（HOI）动作重定向方法，可在不同人形角色间迁移 HOI 动作，同时保持动作语义与一致的接触交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作重定向方法主要只迁移人体动作本身，忽略了人与被操作物体之间的交互一致性。本文目标是在跨异构骨骼角色迁移时，既保留原始身体动作语义，又维持角色与物体之间一致的交互关系，并联合生成对齐的目标人体动作与物体动作。

Method: 基于 Part-Aware Motion Embedding（PAME）自编码器，将全身动作编码到共享的分部位隐空间，以泛化异构骨骼并保留局部动作语义。在其上引入接触引导重定向模块与物体动作解码器：前者将源物体交互作为条件，把物体与接触相关信号编码为与身体部位对齐的隐表示，通过残差控制分支注入解码过程，在不丢弃底层动作先验的前提下增强交互相关部位的适应；后者预测与精炼后目标角色动作对齐的目标物体动作。

Result: ReCHOIR 能够将源 HOI 序列重定向到具有不同骨骼配置的目标角色，在保持动作语义的同时维持接触一致的交互模式，并联合产生对齐的目标人体与物体动作。

Conclusion: 通过分部位隐空间表示与接触引导的残差控制、配合物体动作解码，ReCHOIR 实现了跨异构角色的 HOI 动作重定向，兼顾运动语义与交互一致性，弥补了以往仅迁移人体动作的不足。

Abstract: We present ReCHOIR, a novel contact-guided motion retargeting method for transferring human object interaction (HOI) motions across diverse humanoid characters. Unlike prior motion retargeting methods that primarily focus on transferring human motion alone, our goal is to preserve not only the semantics of the original body movement but also consistent interaction between the character and the manipulated object, while jointly producing aligned target human and object motions. Given source HOI motion, object geometry, and contact cues extracted from the source interaction, ReCHOIR retargets an HOI sequence to target characters with different skeletal configurations while maintaining both motion semantics and contact-consistent interaction patterns. Our method builds on a Part-Aware Motion Embedding (PAME) autoencoder, which encodes full-body motion into a shared body-part-wise latent space. This representation enables generalization across heterogeneous skeletons while preserving local motion semantics beneficial for part-aware adaptation in HOI retargeting. On top of this representation, we introduce a contact-guided retargeting module and an object motion decoder for HOI retargeting. The contact-guided retargeting module treats the source object interaction as a condition for refining target character motion: object- and contact-related signals are encoded into a body-part-aligned latent representation and injected into decoding through a residual control branch, enabling stronger adaptation in interaction-relevant body regions without discarding the underlying motion prior. In parallel, the object motion decoder predicts a target object motion aligned with the refined target character motion, ensuring that the object trajectory remains consistent with how the interaction is realized by the target character.

</details>


### [213] [Learning Realistic Athletic Sprinting Without Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11083)
*William Wang,Nicholas Bianco,Guy Tevet,Jennifer Hicks,C. Karen Liu,Scott Delp,Kayvon Fatahalian*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出一个无需动作示范的肌肉驱动仿真系统，在高性能GPU模拟器上通过大规模强化学习训练控制策略，可在单GPU数小时内生成接近视觉真实的短跑等高速田径运动动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速田径运动（如百米冲刺）的肌肉驱动动作生成具有挑战性：传统方法依赖动作捕捉或示范数据，成本高且难以覆盖高速、高维度肌肉激励空间；同时生物力学模型的仿真速度慢，难以支撑大规模强化学习训练。作者希望构建一个无需示范、又能保持生物力学准确性的动作生成框架。

Method: 将先进生物力学运动员模型集成到新的高性能GPU仿真器中，仿真速度达实时的1000倍。基于该高吞吐仿真，进行大批量强化学习，直接在高维肌肉激励空间中训练控制策略；策略仅由任务特定的回合终止条件和奖励引导，奖励鼓励在满足关节限位约束的前提下最大化速度并降低所需力。

Result: 策略可在单块GPU上数小时内完成训练，生成"接近视觉真实"的完整运动活动，如完整100米短跑以及侧滑步、倒退跑、卡里奥卡步等流行田径移动练习；所生成的冲刺动作与从短跑运动员采集的实验数据高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，结合高速GPU仿真与大规模强化学习，可以无需动作示范地生成生物力学准确的高速运动动作，并能在合理计算成本下扩展到完整运动项目，且结果与真实实验数据一致。

Abstract: We present a muscle-driven simulation system for generating biomechanically accurate motion for high-speed athletic locomotion tasks that does not require motion demonstrations. Our approach integrates state-of-the-art biomechanical athlete models into a new, high-performance GPU simulator capable of running at 1000x real-time. High-throughput simulation enables large-batch reinforcement learning to train control policies that operate directly in the model's high-dimensional muscle excitation space, and are guided only by task-specific episode termination conditions and a reward that encourages maximizing speed while reducing forces needed to respect joint limits. These policies train within a few hours on a single GPU and generate "near visually realistic" motions for complete athletic activities such as a full 100-meter sprint or performing popular athletic locomotion drills like side-shuffling, backpedaling, and carioca. The generated sprinting motions also exhibit strong agreement with experimental data captured from sprinters.

</details>


### [214] [Gaussian Light Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11430)
*Patrick Attimont,Kartic Subr,Cyril Soler*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将光传输方程的解表示为覆盖位置、方向、法线与材质属性的13维高斯混合模型，并通过最小化渲染方程残差直接估计模型参数，实现毫秒级、视图无关的全局光照渲染，且内存占用远低于常规神经渲染方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光传输方法多基于Neumann级数求解，存在计算与存储开销大的问题；同时常规神经渲染方法内存需求高、渲染速度受限。作者希望找到一种更紧凑、可实时评估的全局光照表示。

Method: 将光传输方程的解建模为13维高斯混合模型，变量涵盖位置、方向、表面法线和材质属性；不依赖Neumann级数，而是直接通过最小化渲染方程残差来估计模型参数；针对优化与渲染中反复评估高维高斯函数线性组合的问题，引入高效裁剪（culling）策略以保证优化可处理并支持实时渲染。

Result: 在高斯表示中纳入场景属性可显著减少所需函数数量并加速评估；借助裁剪策略使优化可行；最终能够快速生成视图无关的光传输解，渲染时间达到毫秒量级，且内存需求仅为传统神经渲染方法的一小部分。

Conclusion: 将场景属性融入高维高斯混合表示，并结合残差最小化与高效裁剪，可为实现快速、低内存、视图无关的全局光照提供一种有效途径。

Abstract: We present a novel method for computing global illumination by expressing the solution to the light transport equation as a 13D Gaussian mixture model over positions, directions, surface normals, and material properties. We show that including scene properties in the Gaussian representation drastically reduces the number of functions and speeds up evaluation. As opposed to traditional light transport methods based on Neumann series, the parameters of our model are directly estimated by minimizing the residual of the rendering equation. While both optimization and rendering require repeated evaluations of a linear combination of high-dimensional Gaussian functions, we introduce an efficient culling strategy to keep the optimization tractable and produce renderings in real time. Our representation enables to render fast, view-independent solutions to the light transport equation, achieving rendering times on the order of milliseconds, with a fraction of the memory requirements of conventional neural rendering approaches.

</details>


### [215] [Hologram Representation via Quadratic Phase Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11434)
*Haolong Wang,Yicheng Zhan,Kaan Akşit,Simeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出复数二次相位高斯（CVQPG）全息图表示方法，用二维二次相位函数替换2D高斯溅射中的标准2D高斯基元，并引入可学习曲率参数，在全息重建中平均提升PSNR +0.19 dB（RGB）与+0.33 dB（灰度），实现对自然图像中高频段的有效保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D高斯溅射（2D Gaussian Splatting）中使用的标准2D高斯基元通常为实值、相位恒定（零曲率）的表示，无法建模全息图所必需的波前相位调制特性。全息重建对复值波前的相位分布高度敏感，而现有基元在表达能力和参数效率上存在不足，因此需要一种能够显式控制波前曲率的轻量级表示方法。

Method: 提出CVQPG：将2D高斯溅射中的2D高斯基元替换为二维二次相位函数，形成复值基元；在基元上增加可学习的曲率（二次相位）参数，用于调制每个基元的波前形状。在参数总量对齐（equal parameter count）的条件下，与当前最先进的全息重建方法进行对比评估。

Result: 在全息重建任务上平均超越现有最先进方法：RGB图像PSNR提升+0.19 dB，灰度图像提升+0.33 dB。等参数条件下即取得该增益，说明对基元波前进行相位调制是一种高效且轻量的增强方式。频域分析进一步显示，CVQPG成功保留了自然图像的中高频段信息。

Conclusion: 通过以二次相位函数替代恒定相位的高斯基元并引入可学习曲率参数，CVQPG在不增加参数量的前提下显著提升全息重建质量，并改善中高频细节的表达，表明波前调制是提升全息表示能力的一个有效方向。

Abstract: We introduce Complex-Valued Quadratic Phase Gaussian (CVQPG), a novel hologram representation method that replaces standard 2D Gaussian representations used in 2D Gaussian Splatting with 2D quadratic phase functions. CVQPG incorporates additional learnable parameters to control the curvature of these bases. We evaluate our approach against state-of-the-art methods, exceeding the visual quality by +0.19 dB (RGB) and +0.33 dB (grayscale) on average in holographic reconstructions. Specifically, our equal parameter count evaluations show that modulating the primitive's wavefront is an effective and lightweight enhancement for hologram representations. In addition, our frequency domain analysis illustrates that CVQPG has successfully preserved the mid-to-high frequency band of natural images.

</details>
